\title{Algebraic Polynomial Equations of 7R Mechanism via Half-Angle Substitution}
\author{Automated Derivation ($t_i = \tan(\theta_i/2)$)}
\maketitle

\section*{Position Polynomial Equations ($X, Y, Z$)}
By applying Weierstrass substitution $c_i = \frac{1-t_i^2}{1+t_i^2}$ and $s_i = \frac{2t_i}{1+t_i^2}$, and multiplying by the common denominator $\prod (1+t_i^2)$, we obtain pure algebraic polynomials $F(t) = 0$.
